This file contains the outputs for several experiments on ELM integration with a trained S-SSSM ASR model.
Conducting the experiments I followed this workflow:
================================================================================
COMPLETE WORKFLOW: Mamba ELM + KenLM Comparison on 460h S-SSSM Encoder
================================================================================

Prerequisites:
  pip install https://github.com/kpu/kenlm/archive/master.zip --break-system-packages
  
  Files needed in same directory:
    - IKT464_Script_ASR_SSSM_CharMambaLM_v1_3.py (main script)
    - lm_diagnostic.py                              (LM quality tests)
    - SSSM_Checkpoints/best_epoch=48_val_wer=0.197.ckpt
    - lm_checkpoints/elm_mamba_MaxChars-1000000000_d320_L18/  (already trained)

================================================================================
PHASE 1: LM QUALITY DIAGNOSTICS (run first — validates both LMs are working)
================================================================================

# 1.1 Diagnostic on Mamba ELM   -   (Done)
python IKT590_lm_diagnostic.py --model-path lm_checkpoints/elm_mamba_MaxChars-1000000000_d320_L18

# 1.2a Generate character-level text (only needed once)   -   (Done)
python IKT464_Script_ASR_SSSM_CharMambaLM_v1_3.py --mode train_kenlm --lm-hf-dataset openslr/librispeech_lm --lm-max-chars 1000000000

# 1.2b Train n-gram models from that text   -   (Done)
python IKT590_train_char_ngram.py --train-text lm_checkpoints/kenlm/char_level_text.txt --orders 7,10 --output-dir lm_checkpoints/ngram

# 1.3 Diagnostic on KenLM 10-gram   -   (Done)
python IKT590_lm_diagnostic.py --model-path lm_checkpoints/ngram/char_10gram.pkl

# 1.4 Diagnostic on KenLM 7-gram   -   (Done)
python IKT590_lm_diagnostic.py --model-path lm_checkpoints/ngram/char_7gram.pkl

# 1.5a Enhanced LM Diagnostics on Mamba ELM   -   (Done)
python IKT590_lm_diagnostic_v2.py --model-path lm_checkpoints/elm_mamba_MaxChars-1000000000_d320_L18

# 1.5b Enhanced LM Diagnostics on N-gram 10-gram   -   (Done)
python IKT590_lm_diagnostic_v2.py --model-path lm_checkpoints/ngram/char_10gram.pkl

# 1.5c Enhanced LM Diagnostics on N-gram 7-gram   -   (Done)
python IKT590_lm_diagnostic_v2.py --model-path lm_checkpoints/ngram/char_7gram.pkl



================================================================================
PHASE 2.1: MAMBA ELM EXPERIMENTS (460h encoder)
================================================================================

# 2.1 Tune alpha (dev-clean, beam=10)   -   (Done)
python IKT464_Script_ASR_SSSM_CharMambaLM_v1_4.py --mode tune --data-path ../hub_data/librispeech --dataset-config 100h --asr-checkpoint "encoder_checkpoints/S-SSSM/460h/checkpoints_460h_hier_gating_256_42/best_epoch=49_val_wer=0.197.ckpt" --asr-type sssm --elm-path lm_checkpoints/elm_mamba_MaxChars-1000000000_d320_L18 --beam-width 10 --batch-size 64 --exp-name mamba_tune_460h

# 2.2a Full eval at alpha=32 (best from previous sweep)   -   (Done)
python IKT464_Script_ASR_SSSM_CharMambaLM_v1_4.py --mode full_eval --data-path ../hub_data/librispeech --dataset-config 100h --asr-checkpoint "encoder_checkpoints/S-SSSM/460h/checkpoints_460h_hier_gating_256_42/best_epoch=49_val_wer=0.197.ckpt" --asr-type sssm --elm-path lm_checkpoints/elm_mamba_MaxChars-1000000000_d320_L18 --alpha 32.0 --beta 0.0 --beam-width 10 --batch-size 64 --exp-name mamba_eval_460h_a32

# 2.2b Full eval at alpha=100 (best from previous sweep)   -   (Done)
python IKT464_Script_ASR_SSSM_CharMambaLM_v1_4.py --mode full_eval --data-path ../hub_data/librispeech --dataset-config 100h --asr-checkpoint "encoder_checkpoints/S-SSSM/460h/checkpoints_460h_hier_gating_256_42/best_epoch=49_val_wer=0.197.ckpt" --asr-type sssm --elm-path lm_checkpoints/elm_mamba_MaxChars-1000000000_d320_L18 --alpha 100.0 --beta 0.0 --beam-width 10 --batch-size 64 --exp-name mamba_eval_460h_a100

# 2.3 RTFX measurement   -   (Done)
python IKT464_Script_ASR_SSSM_CharMambaLM_v1_4.py --mode rtfx --data-path ../hub_data/librispeech --dataset-config 100h --asr-checkpoint "encoder_checkpoints/S-SSSM/460h/checkpoints_460h_hier_gating_256_42/best_epoch=49_val_wer=0.197.ckpt" --asr-type sssm --elm-path lm_checkpoints/elm_mamba_MaxChars-1000000000_d320_L18 --alpha 32.0 --beam-width 10 --batch-size 64 --exp-name mamba_rtfx_460h

# 2.4 Hallucination analysis   -   (Done)
python IKT464_Script_ASR_SSSM_CharMambaLM_v1_4.py --mode hallucination --data-path ../hub_data/librispeech --dataset-config 100h --asr-checkpoint "encoder_checkpoints/S-SSSM/460h/checkpoints_460h_hier_gating_256_42/best_epoch=49_val_wer=0.197.ckpt" --asr-type sssm --elm-path lm_checkpoints/elm_mamba_MaxChars-1000000000_d320_L18 --batch-size 64 --exp-name mamba_hallucination_460h

# 2.5 Score analysis (detailed breakdown with PDF figures)   -   (Done)
python IKT464_Script_ASR_SSSM_CharMambaLM_v1_4.py --mode score_analysis --data-path ../hub_data/librispeech --dataset-config 100h --asr-checkpoint "encoder_checkpoints/S-SSSM/460h/checkpoints_460h_hier_gating_256_42/best_epoch=49_val_wer=0.197.ckpt" --asr-type sssm --elm-path lm_checkpoints/elm_mamba_MaxChars-1000000000_d320_L18 --alpha 32.0 --beam-width 10 --batch-size 64 --exp-name mamba_score_analysis_460h

    Typical combined score:      -46.07

    CTC spread:                  9.2324

    ELM × alpha spread:          6.0761
    Ratio (ELM×alpha / CTC):     65.8%

  Re-ranking summary:
    Utterances processed:        2620
    Re-ranked by ELM:            913 (34.8%)
    Confirmed CTC choice:        1707 (65.2%)

  How to read the combined score:
    combined = CTC_score + alpha * (ELM_total / |hypothesis|)
    Example: -0.881 + 32.0 × (-1.1237) = -36.840
    The CTC score is a large negative number (sum of log-probs over ~100-500 frames).
    The ELM score is a small negative number (avg log-prob per character, ~20-100 chars).
    Alpha=32.0 amplifies the ELM so it can influence the ranking despite its small scale.

  Re-ranking summary:
    Utterances processed:        2620
    Re-ranked by ELM:            933 (35.6%)
    Confirmed CTC choice:        1687 (64.4%)

  How to read the combined score:
    combined = CTC_score + alpha * (ELM_total / |hypothesis|)
    Example: -0.923 + 32.0 × (-1.0836) = -35.598
    The CTC score is a large negative number (sum of log-probs over ~100-500 frames).
    The ELM score is a small negative number (avg log-prob per character, ~20-100 chars).
    Alpha=32.0 amplifies the ELM so it can influence the ranking despite its small scale.

    CTC spread:                  9.2324
    ELM/len spread:              0.5112
    ELM × alpha spread:          16.3594

  Re-ranking summary:
    Utterances processed:        2620
    Re-ranked by ELM:            1085 (41.4%)
    Confirmed CTC choice:        1535 (58.6%)

  How to read the combined score:
    combined = CTC_score + alpha * (ELM_total / |hypothesis|)
    Example: -0.881 + 32.0 × (-1.1956) = -39.141
    The CTC score is a large negative number (sum of log-probs over ~100-500 frames).
    The ELM score is a small negative number (avg log-prob per character, ~20-100 chars).
    Alpha=32.0 amplifies the ELM so it can influence the ranking despite its small scale.

    ELM × alpha spread:          9.2274

  Re-ranking summary:
    Utterances processed:        2620
    Re-ranked by ELM:            959 (36.6%)
    Confirmed CTC choice:        1661 (63.4%)

  How to read the combined score:
    combined = CTC_score + alpha * (ELM_total / |hypothesis|)
    Example: -0.881 + 32.0 × (-1.1546) = -37.827
    The CTC score is a large negative number (sum of log-probs over ~100-500 frames).
    The ELM score is a small negative number (avg log-prob per character, ~20-100 chars).
    Alpha=32.0 amplifies the ELM so it can influence the ranking despite its small scale.
